{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BZ44ic5yvh8USVdEZpucGo","type":"tool_result","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgaepS7hLQ41YL6Awmwyi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CBWtqX9XZ3pvhSjnCAezko","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > \"$PWD/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp\" <<'EOF'\n// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy (CUDA):\n//   * Every unclustered point is tried as a seed by its own thread block.\n//   * Inside a block, candidate points are distributed over the threads. Each\n//     thread keeps the running \"max distance to the current cluster\" of its\n//     candidates in registers, so adding a member costs O(M / blockDim) work\n//     plus one block-wide (min value, min index) reduction per growth step.\n//   * Member lists of all candidate clusters of a batch are kept on the\n//     device; a small selection kernel picks the largest one (ties -> earliest\n//     seed, exactly like the sequential code) and keeps it as the running best.\n//   * The host only receives the winning cluster per outer iteration, marks\n//     its members as clustered and compacts the remaining points.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,    \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                       \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\n__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconstexpr int GROW_THREADS = 512;                 // threads per seed block\nconstexpr int GROW_WARPS = GROW_THREADS / 32;\nconstexpr double DEV_INF = 1.0e300;               // \"excluded candidate\" marker\n\n// Combine two (value, index) candidates: smaller value wins, ties -> smaller index.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void betterOf(double& v, int& i, const double ov, const int oi) {\n    if (ov < v || (ov == v && oi < i)) {\n        v = ov;\n        i = oi;\n    }\n}\n\n// Block-wide argmin of (value, index). All threads receive the result.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void blockArgMin(double& v, int& i, double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;\n    const int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n        const double ov = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, v, off);\n        const int oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, i, off);\n        betterOf(v, i, ov, oi);\n    }\n    if (lane == 0) {\n        s_val[warp] = v;\n        s_idx[warp] = i;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (warp == 0) {\n        v = (lane < GROW_WARPS) ? s_val[lane] : DEV_INF;\n        i = (lane < GROW_WARPS) ? s_idx[lane] : -1;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            const double ov = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, v, off);\n            const int oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, i, off);\n            betterOf(v, i, ov, oi);\n        }\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            s_val[0] = v;\n            s_idx[0] = i;\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    v = s_val[0];\n    i = s_idx[0];\n    __syncthreads();   // protect s_val/s_idx before the next reduction reuses them\n}\n\n// Grow one candidate cluster per block. Block b uses seed (seedBase + b).\n//\n// PT > 0 : each thread keeps PT running max-distances in registers.\n// PT == 0: running max-distances live in a per-block global workspace.\n//\n// members[b * M + k] receives the k-th member (local index) of the cluster,\n// cards[b] its cardinality.\ntemplate <int PT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GROW_THREADS)\ngrowClustersKernel(const Point* __restrict__ pts,\n                   const int M,\n                   const int seedBase,\n                   const double threshold,\n                   int* __restrict__ members,\n                   int* __restrict__ cards,\n                   double* __restrict__ workspace) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[GROW_WARPS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[GROW_WARPS];\n\n    const int slot = blockIdx.x;\n    const int seed = seedBase + slot;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    int* __restrict__ myMembers = members + static_cast<size_t>(slot) * M;\n    double* __restrict__ gmax = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(slot) * M;\n\n    constexpr int NLOC = (PT > 0) ? PT : 1;\n    double maxd[NLOC];\n\n    // Step 0: the seed is the only member. Initialise max distances.\n    Point mp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + seed) == double2{} ? Point{0, 0} : Point{0, 0};\n    {\n        const double2 sp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + seed);\n        mp.x = sp.x;\n        mp.y = sp.y;\n    }\n\n    double bestV = DEV_INF;\n    int bestI = -1;\n\n    if (PT > 0) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < NLOC; ++k) {\n            const int c = tid + k * GROW_THREADS;\n            double d = DEV_INF;\n            if (c < M && c != seed) {\n                const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n                d = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n                if (d < threshold && d < bestV) {\n                    bestV = d;\n                    bestI = c;\n                }\n            }\n            maxd[k] = d;\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (int c = tid; c < M; c += GROW_THREADS) {\n            double d = DEV_INF;\n            if (c != seed) {\n                const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n                d = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n                if (d < threshold && d < bestV) {\n                    bestV = d;\n                    bestI = c;\n                }\n            }\n            gmax[c] = d;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) myMembers[0] = seed;\n    int count = 1;\n\n    // Iteratively add the closest admissible point.\n    while (true) {\n        blockArgMin(bestV, bestI, s_val, s_idx);\n        if (bestI < 0) break;   // no candidate keeps the diameter below threshold\n\n        const int newMember = bestI;\n        if (tid == 0) myMembers[count] = newMember;\n        ++count;\n        if (count >= M) break;  // everything is in the cluster\n\n        {\n            const double2 np = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + newMember);\n            mp.x = np.x;\n            mp.y = np.y;\n        }\n\n        bestV = DEV_INF;\n        bestI = -1;\n\n        if (PT > 0) {\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < NLOC; ++k) {\n                const int c = tid + k * GROW_THREADS;\n                double d = maxd[k];\n                if (c < M && d < threshold) {   // excluded / too-far candidates stay excluded\n                    if (c == newMember) {\n                        d = DEV_INF;\n                    } else {\n                        const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n                        const double nd = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n                        d = (nd > d) ? nd : d;\n                        if (d < threshold && d < bestV) {\n                            bestV = d;\n                            bestI = c;\n                        }\n                    }\n                    maxd[k] = d;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int c = tid; c < M; c += GROW_THREADS) {\n                double d = gmax[c];\n                if (d < threshold) {\n                    if (c == newMember) {\n                        d = DEV_INF;\n                    } else {\n                        const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n                        const double nd = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n                        d = (nd > d) ? nd : d;\n                        if (d < threshold && d < bestV) {\n                            bestV = d;\n                            bestI = c;\n                        }\n                    }\n                    gmax[c] = d;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) cards[slot] = count;\n}\n\n// Pick the largest cluster of the batch (ties -> earliest seed) and, if it is\n// strictly larger than the running best, make it the running best.\n// best[0] = cardinality, best[1] = seed (local index), bestMembers = members.\nconstexpr int SELECT_THREADS = 1024;\n\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(SELECT_THREADS)\nselectBestKernel(const int* __restrict__ cards,\n                 const int* __restrict__ members,\n                 const int batch,\n                 const int M,\n                 const int seedBase,\n                 int* __restrict__ best,\n                 int* __restrict__ bestMembers) {\n    __shared__ int s_card[SELECT_THREADS / 32];\n    __shared__ int s_slot[SELECT_THREADS / 32];\n    __shared__ int s_winner;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid & 31;\n    const int warp = tid >> 5;\n\n    int bc = -1, bs = 0x7fffffff;\n    for (int s = tid; s < batch; s += SELECT_THREADS) {\n        const int c = cards[s];\n        if (c > bc || (c == bc && s < bs)) {\n            bc = c;\n            bs = s;\n        }\n    }\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n        const int oc = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, bc, off);\n        const int os = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, bs, off);\n        if (oc > bc || (oc == bc && os < bs)) {\n            bc = oc;\n            bs = os;\n        }\n    }\n    if (lane == 0) {\n        s_card[warp] = bc;\n        s_slot[warp] = bs;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (warp == 0) {\n        bc = (lane < SELECT_THREADS / 32) ? s_card[lane] : -1;\n        bs = (lane < SELECT_THREADS / 32) ? s_slot[lane] : 0x7fffffff;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            const int oc = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, bc, off);\n            const int os = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, bs, off);\n            if (oc > bc || (oc == bc && os < bs)) {\n                bc = oc;\n                bs = os;\n            }\n        }\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            // Strictly greater, like the sequential code: earlier seeds win ties.\n            if (bc > best[0]) {\n                best[0] = bc;\n                best[1] = seedBase + bs;\n                s_winner = bs;\n            } else {\n                s_winner = -1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    const int w = s_winner;\n    if (w >= 0) {\n        const int n = best[0];\n        const int* src = members + static_cast<size_t>(w) * M;\n        for (int k = tid; k < n; k += SELECT_THREADS) {\n            bestMembers[k] = src[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Device buffers reused across outer iterations.\nstruct DeviceState {\n    Point* pts = nullptr;         // compacted unclustered points\n    int* members = nullptr;       // batch * M member lists\n    int* cards = nullptr;         // batch cardinalities\n    double* workspace = nullptr;  // batch * M running max distances (PT==0 only)\n    int* best = nullptr;          // {cardinality, seed}\n    int* bestMembers = nullptr;   // M\n    size_t membersCap = 0;\n    size_t workspaceCap = 0;\n    int ptsCap = 0;\n};\n\nstatic void launchGrow(const int pt, const Point* pts, const int M, const int seedBase,\n                       const double threshold, const int batch, int* members, int* cards,\n                       double* workspace) {\n    const dim3 grid(batch), block(GROW_THREADS);\n    switch (pt) {\n        case 1:  growClustersKernel<1><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        case 2:  growClustersKernel<2><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        case 4:  growClustersKernel<4><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        case 8:  growClustersKernel<8><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        case 16: growClustersKernel<16><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        case 32: growClustersKernel<32><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n        default: growClustersKernel<0><<<grid, block>>>(pts, M, seedBase, threshold, members, cards, workspace); break;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    DeviceState dev;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.pts, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.best, 2 * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.bestMembers, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    dev.ptsCap = N;\n\n    // Device memory budget for per-batch member lists / workspaces.\n    size_t freeMem = 0, totalMem = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&freeMem, &totalMem));\n    const size_t budget = std::max<size_t>(freeMem / 2, static_cast<size_t>(64) << 20);\n\n    std::vector<Point> compact;\n    std::vector<int> hostMembers;\n    compact.reserve(N);\n    hostMembers.reserve(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int M = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Upload the compacted point set (local index i <-> unclustered_indices[i]).\n        compact.resize(M);\n        for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) compact[i] = points[unclustered_indices[i]];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dev.pts, compact.data(), static_cast<size_t>(M) * sizeof(Point),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Register-resident variant when the candidate set is small enough.\n        int pt = 0;\n        for (int cand : {1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32}) {\n            if (M <= cand * GROW_THREADS) { pt = cand; break; }\n        }\n        const size_t perSeedBytes = static_cast<size_t>(M) * sizeof(int) +\n                                    (pt == 0 ? static_cast<size_t>(M) * sizeof(double) : 0);\n        int batch = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(M, std::max<size_t>(1, budget / perSeedBytes)));\n\n        const size_t needMembers = static_cast<size_t>(batch) * M;\n        if (needMembers > dev.membersCap) {\n            if (dev.members) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dev.members));\n            if (dev.cards) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dev.cards));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.members, needMembers * sizeof(int)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.cards, static_cast<size_t>(batch) * sizeof(int)));\n            dev.membersCap = needMembers;\n        }\n        if (pt == 0 && needMembers > dev.workspaceCap) {\n            if (dev.workspace) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dev.workspace));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.workspace, needMembers * sizeof(double)));\n            dev.workspaceCap = needMembers;\n        }\n\n        const int init[2] = {-1, -1};\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dev.best, init, sizeof(init), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in batches of thread blocks.\n        for (int seedBase = 0; seedBase < M; seedBase += batch) {\n            const int b = std::min(batch, M - seedBase);\n            launchGrow(pt, dev.pts, M, seedBase, threshold, b, dev.members, dev.cards, dev.workspace);\n            selectBestKernel<<<1, SELECT_THREADS>>>(dev.cards, dev.members, b, M, seedBase,\n                                                    dev.best, dev.bestMembers);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n\n        int bestInfo[2];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(bestInfo, dev.best, sizeof(bestInfo), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        const int max_cardinality = bestInfo[0];\n        const int best_seed_local = bestInfo[1];\n\n        if (best_seed_local < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {\n            break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n        }\n\n        if (max_cardinality == 1) {\n            // No remaining point has any neighbour within the threshold, so every\n            // remaining point becomes a singleton cluster, in index order.\n            for (int i = 0; i < M; ++i) {\n                Cluster cluster;\n                cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[i];\n                cluster.members.push_back(unclustered_indices[i]);\n                clusters.push_back(cluster);\n                clustered[unclustered_indices[i]] = true;\n            }\n            unclustered_indices.clear();\n            break;\n        }\n\n        hostMembers.resize(max_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostMembers.data(), dev.bestMembers,\n                              static_cast<size_t>(max_cardinality) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_seed_local];\n        cluster.members.resize(max_cardinality);\n        for (int i = 0; i < max_cardinality; ++i) {\n            cluster.members[i] = unclustered_indices[hostMembers[i]];\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n        \n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            clustered[cluster.members[i]] = true;\n        }\n        \n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < Every growth step then\n// touches only that list: O(live / blockDim) work per thread plus one\n// block-wide (min value, min index) reduction.\n//\n// members[b * M + k] receives the k-th member (local index) of the cluster,\n// cards[b] its cardinality. The kernel only processes slots whose cards entry\n// was set to -1 by the warp kernel.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GROW_THREADS)\ngrowClustersBlockKernel(const Point* __restrict__ pts,\n                        const int M,\n                        const int seedBase,\n                        const double threshold,\n                        int* __restrict__ members,\n                        int* __restrict__ cards,\n                        double* __restrict__ wsD,\n                        int* __restrict__ wsI) {\n    __shared__ double s_val[GROW_WARPS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[GROW_WARPS];\n    __shared__ int s_cnt[GROW_WARPS];\n\n    const int slot = blockIdx.x;\n    if (cards[slot] != -1) return;   // already handled by the warp kernel\n    const int seed = seedBase + slot;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid & 31;\n    const int warp = tid >> 5;\n    const unsigned lanemask_lt = (1u << lane) - 1u;\n    int* __restrict__ myMembers = members + static_cast<size_t>(slot) * M;\n    double* __restrict__ d = wsD + static_cast<size_t>(slot) * M;\n    int* __restrict__ ix = wsI + static_cast<size_t>(slot) * M;\n\n    Point mp;\n    {\n        const double2 sp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + seed);\n        mp.x = sp.x;\n        mp.y = sp.y;\n    }\n\n    // Scan all candidates; compact those within threshold of the seed.\n    int n = 0;\n    double bestV = DEV_INF;\n    int bestI = -1;\n    for (int base = 0; base < M; base += GROW_THREADS) {\n        const int c = base + tid;\n        bool alive = false;\n        double dist = 0.0;\n        if (c < M && c != seed) {\n            const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n            dist = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n            alive = dist < threshold;\n        }\n        const unsigned mask = __ballot_sync(0xffffffffu, alive);\n        if (lane == 0) s_cnt[warp] = __popc(mask);\n        __syncthreads();\n        int prefix = 0, total = 0;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int w = 0; w < GROW_WARPS; ++w) {\n            const int cw = s_cnt[w];\n            prefix += (w < warp) ? cw : 0;\n            total += cw;\n        }\n        if (alive) {\n            const int pos = n + prefix + __popc(mask & lanemask_lt);\n            d[pos] = dist;\n            ix[pos] = c;\n            if (dist < bestV) {\n                bestV = dist;\n                bestI = c;\n            }\n        }\n        n += total;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) myMembers[0] = seed;\n    int count = 1;\n\n    // Iteratively add the closest admissible point.\n    while (true) {\n        blockArgMin(bestV, bestI, s_val, s_idx);\n        if (bestI < 0) break;   // no candidate keeps the diameter below threshold\n\n        const int newMember = bestI;\n        if (tid == 0) myMembers[count] = newMember;\n        ++count;\n\n        {\n            const double2 np = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + newMember);\n            mp.x = np.x;\n            mp.y = np.y;\n        }\n\n        bestV = DEV_INF;\n        bestI = -1;\n        for (int j = tid; j < n; j += GROW_THREADS) {\n            double v = d[j];\n            if (v < threshold) {   // excluded / too-far candidates stay excluded\n                const int c = ix[j];\n                if (c == newMember) {\n                    v = DEV_INF;\n                } else {\n                    const double2 cp = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const double2*>(pts) + c);\n                    const double nd = distance(Point{cp.x, cp.y}, mp);\n                    v = (nd > v) ? nd : v;\n                    if (v < threshold && v < bestV) {\n                        bestV = v;\n                        bestI = c;\n                    }\n                }\n                d[j] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (tid == 0) cards[slot] = count;\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:start] + new_block + s[end:]\n\n# launchGrow\nstart = deliberately slightly conservative.\nconstexpr int DIRTY_THREADS = 256;\n\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(DIRTY_THREADS)\nmarkDirtyKernel(const Point* __restrict__ allPts,\n                const int* __restrict__ unclIdx,\n                const int M,\n                const int* __restrict__ removed,\n                const int k,\n                const double threshold,\n                unsigned char* __restrict__ dirty) {\n    __shared__ double2 s_rem[DIRTY_THREADS];\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * DIRTY_THREADS + threadIdx.x;\n    Point p{0.0, 0.0};\n    if (i < M) {\n        const 1e-9);\n    for (int base = 0; base < k; base += DIRTY_THREADS) {\n        const int j = base + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < k) {\n            s_rem[threadIdx.x] = __ldg(reinterpret_cast<const ++t) {\n                const double2 r = s_rem[t];\n                if (distance(p, Point{r.x, r.y}) < limit) {\n                    isDirty = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n                              cards, wsD, wsI);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n }\n\n    DeviceState dev;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.allPts, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.pts, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dev.bestMembers, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dev.allPts, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),\n const size_t budget = std::max<size_t>(freeMem / 2, static_cast<size_t>(64) << 20);\n\n    // Cached cardinality of the candidate cluster of every seed (original\n    // index). clusters.push_back(cluster);\n        \n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            clustered[cluster.members[i]] = true;\n static_cast<size_t>(max_cardinality) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        markDirtyKernel<<<(M + DIRTY_THREADS - 1) / DIRTY_THREADS, DIRTY_THREADS>>>(\n The QT clustering benchmark now runs unconditionally on the GPU with CUDA, producing bit-identical cluster membership to the original sequential code.\n\n**Files changed** (no new files, executable name unchanged):\n\n- `qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp` is now CUDA source. Data generation, argument parsing, validation, and result printing are untouched. The clustering core was replaced by a GPU implementation.\n- `qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt` enables the CUDA language, compiles the .cpp file as CUDA via a source property, and targets the native GPU architecture.\n\n**How the parallel version works**\n\n- Every unclustered point is grown as a seed by its own warp. Only points within the threshold of the seed can ever join, so the warp compacts those live candidates into shared memory and shrinks the list each step, so each growth step needs only a warp shuffle reduction and no block-wide sync.\n- Seeds with more than 1024 neighbours fall back to a block-per-seed kernel that keeps the compacted candidate list in global memory.\n- A seed's greedy cluster depends only on the unclustered points within its threshold, so cardinalities and member lists are cached per seed. After a cluster is removed, a small kernel flags only the seeds whose neighbourhood lost a point, and only those are recomputed. This preserves exact semantics while cutting work by an order of magnitude at large N.\n- Tie-breaking matches the original exactly: smallest distance then lowest index when growing, and largest cardinality then earliest seed when selecting. The singleton shortcut when the max cardinality is 1 is provably equivalent.\n\n**Verification**.
